- `BrowserViewModel.Selection` holds it; `SelectedIndex` is now the focused index (the setter selects that book alone, -1 clears), `SelectedItem` the focused item (Enter and double click open it), `SelectedBooks` the Core books in grid order (what commands use), `SelectedCount`, event `SelectionChanged`, commands `SelectAllCommand`, `InvertSelectionCommand`, `RemoveSelectedCommand` and event `RemoveBooksRequested` (P5.5 answers it). A search keeps the selected books it hides (they come back when the search is cleared, as the original keeps the state of items that are not displayed) and a refresh or another list forgets them; the status text counts the selection (`SelectionInfo`: "- 3 selected / 4,50 MB", the file of a single book).
- Grid (`BookGridView`): pointer as `ItemView.OnMouseDownView`: a press on an unselected book acts at once, on a selected one at the release (it could start a drag); a context click selects an unselected book and keeps the selection otherwise; a press on the empty area clears (not on a scroll bar); a double click opens the focused book. Keys: arrows, Home, End, Page Up and Down move the focus (Up, Down and the page keys keep the column and stop in the first or last row, as `GetRelativeItem`; the MVP jumped to the first or last book), Space, Option+Space, Cmd+A (only while the grid has the focus, so text fields keep theirs), Delete and Cmd+Backspace. `BookCell.SetState(selected, focused)`: the focused book of a multi selection is drawn as selected and hot (255/63), the others as selected (164/41), a focused unselected book with the frame only (the existing `StyledSelection` states).
- `ui-drive click` has `--cmd` and `--shift` (flags on the mouse events). e2e case `grid-multiselect` (status text checks; click points are those of the default 1100 x 748 window with 5 columns). Tests: `Browser/SelectionTests` (model, view model, grid with keys and clicks).

**Facts from RV6 (2026-10-21)**
- Original rules read from `ItemView.cs` (read only): `OnKeyDown` takes `displayedItems[0]` when nothing has the focus, so the first Right selects the second book, Left and Up stay on the first, Down goes to row 1 of column 0, and Space selects the first book without focusing it (only the movement keys call `Focus`); a mouse press scrolls nothing but the release does (`OnMouseUpView`: `EnsureItemVisible(FocusedItem)`). `UpdateSelectionFromMouse(null)` (a press on no item) clears the selection and the focus unless Shift or Ctrl is held, for any button, and records the press point and the state (the band); `UpdateSelection` runs on every move and on every scroll: the band rectangle is kept in content coordinates (it grows when the view scrolls), only the items that intersect the viewport are re-evaluated (an item scrolled out of view keeps the state it had), an item is reset to the state at the press when Shift or Ctrl is held now (else to unselected) and then selected (flipped with Ctrl) if the band touches it (`Rectangle.IntersectsWith`: strict, a zero width band inside an item counts), the focus is cleared and goes to the item under the pointer. `OnPaint`: the band is filled with `Highlight` at alpha 128 two units inside and framed by a `Highlight` pen (GDI+ draws it one unit wider and higher). `ScrollControl` (Drag mode): a left press starts a 20 ms timer, the content moves by the distance of the pointer beyond the control edge (10 and a tenth of the rest above 10). `OnItemDrag` fires after the pointer moved more than `SystemInformation.DragSize` (4) from the press, for any button.
- What is an item: `CoverViewItem.Contains` and `IntersectsWith` test the rectangle `ThumbIconRenderer.Draw` returned, the cover picture's rectangle together with the caption text as laid out, not the whole cell. `CrThumbnailItem.HitRegion` and `HitTest` are that (item coordinates, right and bottom edges excluded); the 4 unit border, the shadow and the free part of the caption area are the empty area of the view. The hot state still follows the whole cell (pointer enter and exit): section 9.
- Code: `Browser/SelectionModel` (`BeginMarquee`, `UpdateMarquee(visible, hit, pointed, keepBase, flip)`, `EndMarquee`, `IsMarqueeActive`; no event when a band move changed nothing; `Space` no longer sets the focus), `Controls/CrSelectionRectangle` (chrome layer, element `ItemView`, theme `CrSelectionRectangle.Original`; `Shapes(band, out fill, out frame)`), `Views/BookGridView` (content is a `Grid` of the scroll viewer and the band; `Band`, `IsMarqueeActive`, `MarqueeRectangle`, `AdvanceAutoScroll()` and the static `AutoScrollEnabled` (the app turns the 20 ms timer on, a test calls `AdvanceAutoScroll`), `DragThreshold`, `DragStarter`, `DragEffects`). Content coordinates of the band are those of the repeater: viewport point plus scroll offset minus the repeater margin; the pointer is captured while the band is down (moves outside the window still count); `ApplySelection` does not scroll to the focused book while a band is down.
- Drag out: `Browser/BookDrag.cs` (`BookDragRequest`, `IBookDragStarter`, `AvaloniaBookDragStarter`, `BookDrag.BooksFormat` = in-process `DataFormat<ComicBookContainer>` named `ComicRack.ComicBookContainer`, `Container`, `Files` (linked books), `Create`). **The system's drag session ends the process with an `NSGenericException` ("N items on the pasteboard, but M drag images") when the transfer has an item that is not a pasteboard item** (found on the real screen): the in-process format therefore rides on the first file item, and without files one text item carries it (`BookDrag.Text`). A drag of the 50 fixture books works on screen (`Drag started: 50 books, 50 files`). Files come from `TopLevel.StorageProvider.TryGetFileFromPathAsync` (complete at once on the desktop); the log lines `Drag started: N books, M files` and `Drag ended: <effect>` are what the e2e case reads. P5.16c reads `BookDrag.BooksFormat` from the drop and adds Move.
- ui-drive: `drag <x1> <y1> <x2> <y2> [--cmd] [--shift] [--steps N] [--hold-ms N]` (WindowTool `drag`: left button down, `leftMouseDragged` events, hold, up; the click safety checks for both points). e2e case `grid-marquee` passed on an unlocked screen: bands with no modifier, Shift and Cmd (status text), the pure Highlight probe pixel of the band's left line in a screenshot taken while the button is held (scale 2), a real drag session of one book and of all fifty. Shots: `mac/artifacts/e2e/grid-marquee/`.
- Tests: `Browser/RubberBandTests` (17: model rules; grid with real pointer events: press in the cover border starts a band, the band selects what it touches and focuses the book under the pointer, up-left, Shift, Cmd and shrinking, right button press, no scroll to the focus during the band and a scroll at the release, auto scroll below and above with the step values 19 and 12 and the ends, scroll while the band is down with books out of view keeping their state, pointer outside the window, hit region), `Browser/BookDragOutTests` (6: threshold 4, whole selection in grid order without collapsing, empty area / right button / hover, double click, transfer layout), `HiDpi/RubberBandHiDpiTests` (6: fill and one unit frame at 1x and 2x in light and dark, frame pixels pure, nothing outside; the band over the grid at 1x and 2x with the same logical rectangle). Existing keyboard tests changed for the first key. Mutation check: drag threshold -1, first key from index -1 and a whole-cell hit test together failed 14 tests (restored).

**Facts from RV7 (2026-10-21)**
- `SettingsStore.Load(path, out note, out replaceable)`: only parse errors (`InvalidOperationException` from the serializer, `XmlException`, `InvalidDataException`, `FormatException`) count as a damaged file (copy as `.bad`, defaults, note "could not be read"). `IOException` and `UnauthorizedAccessException` give the defaults and a note "could not be opened" without a copy; a vanished file (`FileNotFoundException`, `DirectoryNotFoundException`) is a missing file. When the file could not be opened, or the `.bad` copy failed, `SettingsStore.SavingBlocked` is true: no save is queued at start and none is ever written during that run (later changes included), so the file keeps its content. `HostService` logs `LoadNote` through the new `AppLog.Warn` (level `WARN`, not counted as an error).
- `SaveNow` serializes under `gate` to a byte array (a sequence number is taken there), writes outside it under `ioGate` (temp file, fsync, rename; `WriteAtomic`) and drops a snapshot older than the one the file holds, so a setter (`QueueSave`) never waits for the disk and an older save never replaces a newer one. `Flush` also waits for a write another thread has in progress. Test seams: `Open(..., writer)` replaces the file write, `AfterSnapshot` (internal; `ComicRack.Mac.Host.Tests` has `InternalsVisibleTo`) runs between the snapshot and the write. A failed write marks the store dirty only when it was the latest snapshot.
- `WindowPlacement.Correct`: when the size was changed (shrunk to the screen, or raised to the minimum), X and Y are clamped as well so that the whole frame is inside the screen it overlaps most (`ClampStart`); a frame whose size is untouched keeps the old rule (a title bar with 100 units visible stays where the user left it). `SidebarViewModel.ClampWidth` (26 to 1000, NaN and infinity give 252) is applied on restore only; the saved value is not rewritten.
- ui-drive `drag`: `--steps` at most 200, `--hold-ms` at most 10000 (shell `DRAG_MAX_STEPS`, `DRAG_MAX_HOLD_MS` and Swift `maxDragSteps`, `maxDragHoldMs`; above the cap or a non-number is exit 2, long digit strings included; a test compares the two sets of numbers). `windowtool drag` re-checks (unlocked, app frontmost, window on screen at the same origin, point inside, topmost normal window is the app's) before the mouse move, before every dragged event, every 50 ms of the hold and before the release; on a failure it posts the mouse-up at the **start** point, puts the cursor back and exits 5; SIGINT, SIGTERM and SIGHUP set a flag that does the same with exit 130. `ui-drive.sh drag` runs the helper as a child and forwards those signals. A drag session of the system (drag out of the grid) passes the re-checks (e2e `grid-marquee` passed). `tests/e2e/lib.sh`: `bg_run cmd...` runs a command in the background and records it (`LAST_BG`), `e2e_cleanup` ends all of them (`e2e_stop_background`: TERM to the children first, then wait); `grid-marquee` uses it for its held drag.
- Tests: `SettingsStoreTests` +4 (unreadable file never overwritten, failed `.bad` copy, a setter during a blocked write, older snapshot dropped), `WindowPlacementTests` +2, `PreferencesTests` sidebar clamp (7 cases), `UiDriveScriptTests` +4 groups (drag and frame refusals, the caps, same numbers in shell and Swift), `E2eScriptTests` +2 (the cleanup kills a background command and its child). Mutation check: the lock held during the write, the sequence test removed, the blocked flag removed, no X/Y clamp and no width clamp each fail the new tests; the old `ui-drive.sh` and `lib.sh` fail the script tests.


**Facts from RV8 (2026-10-21)**
- Toolbar: `CrToolBar` lays its items out with `ToolBarLayout.Compute` (no control; `HorizontalStackWithOverflow`): left items from the left, `CrToolBar.Alignment="Right"` items from the right (the first right item is the rightmost), `CrToolBar.Overflow="Never"` items stay, `Always` items are always in the drop-down; when the `AsNeeded` and `Never` items do not fit, the overflow button (16 wide, `ToolBarLayout.OverflowButtonWidth`) takes the right end and the first `AsNeeded` item that does not fit, and every `AsNeeded` item after it, go to the drop-down. The items in the drop-down stay in the panel (arranged off to the left, clipped by the bar) so their size is known for the next plan; `ShowOverflow()` moves them (the same controls) into a popup list and `HideOverflow()` or the close of the popup gives them back in their order (separators are not moved). The library window's toolbar now has the designer's order (List Layouts after Arrange) with the quick search last in the markup but at the right end (`Alignment.Right`, `Overflow.Never`); at the minimum width 640 with the 252 side panel the Undo and Redo buttons (and the ones before them as needed) overflow. The overflow button is `CrToolBar.OverflowButton` (internal, `Shown` is a render only switch so the layout is not invalidated from the arrange pass), its look `Cr.ToolOverflowButtonGradient*`, the bar over the `SmallArrowDown` image; chrome decision C-TB-2.
- Arrange (`BrowserViewModel`): `SortDescending` (saved as the Mac-only `AppPreferences.SortDescending`), `SortIconName` (`SortUp` ascending, `SortDown` descending), `FlipSortOrder()` (the button click) and `ArrangeBy(index)` (the menu: the sort the list has flips, another sort starts ascending, one query only). A descending list is the query result reversed on the worker. The MVP's "Date added" key is newest first when ascending; P5.14 gives the real arrange keys.
- `NotifyBooksChanged` moves the last-opened marker through `SetLastOpened` (the cells redraw by themselves); it checks the batch against a `HashSet` of the shown books built once. `SelectionModel.SetItems` clears the anchor: the next range starts at the focused book, else the first (`SelectFromAnchorItem`). A context click goes through the same rules as any click when Cmd or Shift is held; without a modifier a selected book keeps the selection.
- Reader: Option+Shift+0 to 5 rate the book in `ReaderViewModel.HandleKey` (the shortcuts of `cmRate0` to `cmRate5`); the page menu has no Info... item until P5.7a; Home, End and the arrows are not handled with Shift (the original's keyboard map matches key and modifiers exactly). `ReaderOptions.ShowVisiblePagePartOverlay` writes through to the preferences it was made from (`ReaderOptions.From`). `CrNavigationOverlay` centres the page strip on the slider's value (`sliderValue`; during a drag the displayed page does not move) and asks `INavigationThumbnailSource.Get(page, pixels)` for pictures at the strip height times the render scaling (device pixels). `CorePageThumbnails` is tested (`CorePageThumbnailsTests`: one reference per caller, 64 kept, an evicted page comes back from the service's cache without a decode, a failed page is not asked for again, a close during a decode leaves no pixels).
- Chrome layer: `Controls/CrReaderPage` (element ReaderOverlay, theme `CrReaderPage.Original`, `IReaderPageSource` implemented by `ReaderViewModel`) draws the reading surface; `Views/ReaderView` derives from it. `Controls/ThumbnailPlaceholderLook` (`Light`, `Dark`, `Current`, `For(variant)`, `Follow(app)`; the entries `Cr.Thumb.PlaceholderTile/Frame/CrossBrush` in both theme dictionaries of `CrThumbnailLook.axaml`) gives `ThumbnailPixels.CreatePlaceholder(height[, look])` its colours; `App` calls `Follow` so the tile follows the appearance (chrome decision C-LIST-3). `NoLiteralColorsTests` scans every folder of the app but `Themes/`, `Platform/` and `Assets/`, with the Skia and short hex forms outside `Controls/`; `ChromeLint.ForbiddenInViews` includes `Avalonia.Media.*`.
- `MacAlert`: `ShowAsync` from another thread is marshalled to the UI thread; `ShowNative` runs in an `ObjC.AutoreleasePool()` (new: `objc_autoreleasePoolPush/Pop`); `MacAlert.ButtonOf(response, options)` maps `runModal` (1000 + the button; Stop -1000, Abort -1001 and anything else answer as the cancel button, or -1 without one, never as the first button).
- Parity details: `MainMenu.Aliases` has Cmd+F9 for `MainViewModel.ShowSettingsCommand` (a not-yet command like Help; the Settings item stays disabled with Cmd+,); `Localization.Match` maps every `zh-Hans*` tag to the `zh-CN` folder (85 files; `zh-Hans` has 57, `zh` 63) when it exists.
- Test strength: `PreferencesTests` checks that nothing is kept without preferences (a second view model has the defaults), `SettingsStoreTests` checks that a save replaces the old content, `LiveRefreshTests` scrolls to the selected cell instead of `if (cell != null)`, `GridHiDpiTests` has 1.5x again for the exact cover (the P5.R2 snapping already makes it pass), `E2eScriptTests` lists all 19 cases and fails when a case is added without being listed. `ChromeSnapshotTests` in `mac/tools/verify.sh --full` (which exports `CRMAC_FULL_GATE=1`) needs the baseline frames in `mac/artifacts/chrome/baseline` (or `CRMAC_CHROME_BASELINE_DIR`) and fails with the command that takes them; RUNBOOK section 4 says so. The baseline of this Mac was taken after RV8 (the toolbar of the library window changed).
- Mutation checks (once): the Arrange click opening the menu again, `lastOpened = newest` without `SetLastOpened`, and the old anchor rule each failed their new test (`The_arrange_button_flips...`, `The_open_and_the_last_opened_book...` (the `Changed` count), `A_new_list_has_no_anchor...`); restored.

**Facts from RV10 (2026-10-21)**
- Full gate on the healthy disk (25 GB free) passed with no code change: `verify.sh --full` 1576 tests, e2e 21 cases (first run lost focus in 4 cases: chrome, grid-multiselect, import, menu-structure; all passed on the single retry), census pass B 0 errors, `upstream-sync-check.sh --no-merge` clean. The `--full` chrome baseline frames existed and matched.

**Facts from RV9 (2026-10-21)**
- The product never announced a host twice: `HostService.Complete` posts `Opened` once per open (start, and again after an import with the new host) and `App` has the only subscription. The double call was test-only: `Launch.Start` subscribed `Opened` and also called `OnHostOpened` itself. `Launch.Announce` now calls it once per host (or failure), whichever comes first (the explicit call serves tests that start off the UI thread, the event serves the reopen after an import); `MainViewModel.HostOpenedRuns` counts the runs. `OnHostOpened` itself stays re-runnable on purpose (tests refresh the library with it).
- `DatabaseManager.Database` (original Core) closes its init wait handle on the first access, so two threads making the first access can hit `ObjectDisposedException`; `MacHost` now makes that first access itself right after `DatabaseManager.Open`, on the opening thread, before the host is handed out.

**Facts from P5.32a (2026-10-21)**
- Structure: `Services/ReaderManager` is the original's `NavigatorManager`: `Slots` (`ReaderSlot`: `Book`, `Model` = its `ReaderViewModel`, `Thumbnails`, `Caption`; an empty slot has no model), `CurrentSlot`/`Current`/`CurrentModel`, events `SlotsChanged`, `CurrentSlotChanged`, `CaptionsChanged`, `OpenBooksChanged`, `ToggleBrowserRequested`, `ToggleUndockRequested`; `Open(book, OpenBookOptions)` (`InNewTab`, `Append`, `NoIncreaseOpenedCount`), `Adopt(model, book)` (tests: a reader made by the caller), `Restore(files)`, `AddSlot`, `Close(index)`, `CloseAll`, `CloseAllButCurrent`, `CloseAllToTheRight`, `Next`, `Previous`, `Move`, `OpenFiles`. The rules are those of `NavigatorManager.Open`: a book that is open is shown (not counted again); otherwise it **takes the place of the book in the current tab** (also an empty tab), unless opened in a new tab, which is **inserted at the left end** (`slots.Insert(0, ...)`, as the original does) or appended for a restored session; closing the current tab shows the tab to its right, else the previous one. The constructor lost its `perf` argument (`new ReaderManager(hostService, preferences)`); `IReaderLauncher` is unchanged (the view model uses the manager directly through `MainViewModel.Tabs`). `Windows` is gone (`Slots`). The log lines `Reader opened:` and `Reader closed: ... on page N.` stay (the e2e cases read them). `ReaderViewModel` got `NextTabRequested`, `PreviousTabRequested`, `ToggleBrowserRequested`, `ToggleUndockRequested` (Tab, Shift+Tab, Esc, D), `RefreshTitle()`; **Esc no longer closes the reader**: it leaves full screen, else asks for the browser dock to open or close (ShowBrowser); Cmd+W (`Close()`) closes the tab. `CoreReadingSession.IncreaseOpenedCount` (false for a restored book).
- Views: `Views/ReaderArea` (UserControl, the page and the overlays of the old `ReaderWindow`; it shows the book of its DataContext, attaches and detaches the model events when it changes, hosts the full screen sync with the window it is in, sets the page menu and handles the keys: `HandleKey`), `Views/ReaderWindow` (a window around a `ReaderArea`, `Display(model, thumbnails)`; the constructor `ReaderWindow(model, ownsModel, perf, thumbnails)` still exists and a window that owns its model closes with it, which the tests use), `Views/MainWindow` (the DockPanel: `StatusBar`, `TabBar` with the reader toolbar in its slot, `BrowserDock` (`CrSizableContainer` Top grip: the old library window content), `ReaderHost` with `ReaderPaneHost` > `ReaderPane` and `QuickOpenPane` (class `cr-reader-paper`); accessors `Tabs`, `ReaderToolbar`, `Reader`, `UndockedReader`). The area has the DataContext of its book, so its `IsVisible` is bound on the wrapper `ReaderPaneHost` (a binding on the area would read the book's view model). `ReaderMenu.cs` is deleted: `MainMenu.BuildMenuBar` holds File > Close, Close All, New Tab, Open Books; Browse > Browser (F3, done early so a closed dock can be reopened); Read > the page commands, Show in Browser, Previous/Next Tab, Track current Page; Display > Page Layout, Zoom, Full Screen, Reader in own Window; the Read and Display items are `MainViewModel.ReaderCommand(m => m.XCommand)` (`CurrentReaderCommand`: disabled without a book) with check marks that follow the current reader (`FollowReader`). The reader window gets its own instance of the same bar. Key aliases: Cmd+W, Cmd+Shift+O. The Open Books items have Ctrl+Cmd+F1 to F12 (Option+Cmd+F3 is Show in Browser).
- `MainViewModel` (partial `MainViewModel.Tabs.cs`): `Reader`, `ReaderChanged`, `ReaderPropertyChanged`, `FocusReaderRequested` (the window gives the page the keyboard focus when a book is opened or a tab chosen), `HasBook`, `ShowReaderArea`, `ShowReaderPage`, `ShowQuickOpen`, `BrowserFills` (no reader, or the reader is in its own window), `IsReaderFullScreen`, `ShowChrome`, `ShowTabBar`, `BrowserDockVisible`, `IsBrowserVisible`, `BrowserDockHeight`, `IsReaderUndocked`, `ToggleBrowser`, `ToggleReaderUndocked`, `CloseBook`, `CloseAllBooks`, `NewTab`, `PreviousTab`, `NextTab`, `CloseAllButThis`, `CloseAllToTheRight`, `RevealInFinder`, `CopyFullPath`, `ShowInBrowser` (`BrowserViewModel.SelectBook`, else the list of `LastOpenedFromListId`, which the open path sets), `ToggleTrackCurrentPage`, `RememberOpenBooks()` (call before the tabs are closed: `App.OnExit`, `Launch.Quit`), session restore once per run in `OnHostOpened`. `BrowserViewModel.OpenSelected(OpenTabMode)` (Default, Invert = Cmd or Shift held, NewTab), `PendingOpenMode`, `OpenSelectedInNewTab`. `IShell` has optional members `RevealInFinder`, `CopyTextAsync`, `ActivateLibrary`, `Quit` (default interface methods; `NullShell` records them). The window title is "ComicRack - <caption>" for a book in a container (every library book), the file name otherwise.
- Settings (`Settings.xml`): `TrackCurrentPage` (mirrored, default true, sets `ComicBookNavigator.TrackCurrentPage`), Mac only `LastOpenBooks` (files in tab order), `BrowserDockHeight` (250), `BrowserDockExpanded` (true), `ReaderUndocked` (false). `OpenLastFile` (PB.02) and `OpenInNewTab` (PB.09) work. `ReaderWindow` is now the frame of the undocked reader window only. Quitting with the reader in its own window keeps it undocked (only the user closing the window docks it: `WindowClosing` reason).
- Chrome layer: `CrTabBar` (tabs with `MinimumWidth`, `AdjustWidth`, `ShowText`, `ToolTipText`, `Selectable`/`Clicked`; scroll arrows, drop-down button, `ScrollBy`, `OpenTabList`, `TabMenu`/`TabMenuOpening`, `OpenTabMenu`; `ComputeLayout` still takes (bar width, slot width) and reserves the 16 unit drop-down width as the original does), `CrSizableContainer` (`Fill`, `ClickTogglesExpanded`: a click that moves less than 4 units on the grip collapses and expands it, as in the original; it also applies to the side panel). Decisions C-MAIN-1, C-TAB-2, C-WIN-4, C-READ-4. A crash found by the real window: `CrTextOverlay.Render` set `MaxTextHeight` to 0 while a window left full screen (unhandled exception, exit 134); it draws nothing now (test `A_text_overlay_with_no_room...`).
- Hidden option `--browser-height N` (26 to 5000, needs a private data folder; saved like a drag of the grip) sets the dock height; e2e `lib.sh` passes `--browser-height 640` (`E2E_BROWSER_HEIGHT`, empty = the default 250) and moves the click points of the grid down by `GRID_DY` 62 (tab strip 31, reader area 25, grip 6); `main_window_id` is the one window (the reader is a tab), `other_window_id` finds dialogs and the undocked reader; the main window title regex is `^ComicRack( - .*)?$`. Cases changed: `grid-keys`, `reader-keys`, `fullscreen` (the window with a book; a second Esc no longer closes it), `reader-look`, `pdf-cb7`, `hidpi`, `menu-structure` (File, Browse, Read, Display items), `grid-marquee`, `grid-multiselect`, `refresh-after-read`, `chrome`, `state-restore` (default dock: an untouched window writes no `Settings.xml`). New case `book-tabs` (three tabs by Cmd+Shift+O, Cmd+Shift+K/J and the Tab key cycle, Close All But This from the tab's menu with the keyboard, F12 undocks and docks, Close All); `E2eScriptTests` lists it. The whole suite passed on the unlocked screen on the final tree: 20 of 20 (an earlier run had 3 failures, all fixed: the overlay crash, `--browser-height` written into the settings of `state-restore`, and one focus loss to another app in `grid-marquee`).
- Tests: `Reader/MainWindowReaderTests` (19, with the harness `MainReaderHarness`: a `MainWindow` over a `ReaderManager` that has no engine, books of generated pages put in by `Adopt`), `Theme/TabBarBehaviourTests` (10), `Theme/SizableContainerTests` (2), `HiDpi/MainWindowReaderHiDpiTests` (6), `ReaderFlowTests` (tabs, Open in new Tab, Cmd/Shift reversal, restore at the next start), `ReaderPreferencesTests` (fit and layout kept), `SettingsStoreTests` (new settings), `ChromeToolingTests` (`--browser-height`), a test for the overlay crash. The reader tests that checked the tab strip, toolbar and status bar of the old reader window moved to `MainWindowReaderTests`; `ReaderHarness` (a `ReaderWindow`) now shows the reading area alone (`ReaderChromeTests.Open` uses 900 x 645 as before so the pixel geometry is unchanged). `ChromeSnapshotTests` has the new frame `main-book-*`; the git-ignored baseline in `mac/artifacts/chrome/baseline` was taken again from this tree (the `reader-*` frames lost the strip and the status bar on purpose). The fast gate: 1530 tests passed (323 s).

**Facts from RV11 (2026-10-21)**
- Baseline before the fix: 6 consecutive `verify.sh` runs gave 1 failure (`SmallPreviewWiringTests.The_browser_selection_feeds_the_preview_through_the_real_session_...`, "Expected: 13, Actual: 0" in `Dispose`: the baseline of the global `PixelBlock.LiveCount` taken in the constructor was still being released) and durations of 209, 319, 340, 291, 290 and 330 s. The spread came from `Gc.SettleLive()` in 15 test class constructors: it waited up to 2 s for a stable global count and hit its limit in about half of the tests when the machine was busy (every `PageServiceTests`, `MainWindowReaderTests`, `ReaderChromeTests` test cost 2.2 to 2.7 s for nothing).
- Fix: each test counts its own pixel blocks. `PixelBlock.Tracker` (a `PixelBlockTracker`, set by test code only; null in the app) is attached to every block made while it is current, and the block reports its release to it. The tests read `Gc.Live` (the running test's count) instead of `PixelBlock.LiveCount`; a class calls `Gc.BeginCount()` (returns 0, no GC, no wait) in its constructor, `TestGuard.Before` starts a count for a test whose class did not; `Gc.WaitForLive(n)` returns at once when the count is right and otherwise flushes and polls (2 s limit). `Ui.BeginTest` and `Ui.LiveBaseline` are gone (the application reset between tests releases blocks of the earlier test's tracker). `Image.StorageTracker` (an `ImageStorageTracker`) does the same for `Image.LiveStorageCount` in `Compat.Drawing.Tests` (`TestImages.Live`, a `TrackImages` assembly attribute; `LiveSettled()` is now `Live`, no GC). Regression tests: `PixelCountIsolationTests`, `ImageCountIsolationTests`.
- `TestGuard` also restores the static test switches to their value at the start of each test (`MacAlert.UseNative`, `CrSizableContainer.EnableAnimation`, `CoverView.FadeEnabled`, `ReaderOverlayClock.Enabled`, `BookGridView.AutoScrollEnabled`) and closes a leaked `MacHost` as before. Shared static state that tests can reach and the guard does not reset: `ViewRegistry.MainWindow/Library` (set by `App` per job), `App.StartupOptions`, `ThumbnailPlaceholderLook.current`, `ChromeVariants`, `AppLog`; no test changes them without restoring today. A new test that changes a static switch adds it to `TestGuard`.
- Rule for new tests (replaces the RV1 rule on baselines): read `Gc.Live`, never `PixelBlock.LiveCount` or `Image.LiveStorageCount`; take no baseline with a garbage collection.
- Gate numbers after RV11: 6 consecutive `verify.sh` runs passed (1619 tests; 89 to 98 s of tests, 91 to 99 s per run with the build). Before: 209 to 340 s. The three `ThreadAbandonTests` that wait for the real 5 s and 10 s limits of the original (`BackgroundRunner.Dispose`, `ComicScanner.Stop` twice) are `Slow` now (25 s; `--full` still runs them; the 200 ms test of `ThreadUtility.Abort` stays in the fast gate). Slowest remaining tests are 1 to 3.4 s (`LanguageRaceTests` fresh process 3.4, `LibraryFlowTests` imports 2.8 and 1.8, `ReaderTimingTests` 1.7, `FixtureGridTests` 1.6); by class: `SyncCheckScriptTests` 7.0 s, `PageServiceTests` 6.9, `LibraryFlowTests` 5.6, `CoreCommandTests` 5.4, `UpstreamScanTests` 4.7.
- `mac/tools/flaky-scan.sh N [verify.sh options]` runs the fast gate N times one after the other, keeps build.log, test.log, verify.log and the .trx files of every run under `mac/artifacts/verify/flaky-<timestamp>/run-<i>/` and prints the result and duration of each run and the failing tests with the number of runs they failed in (exit 0 all passed, 1 a failure, 2 bad arguments, 3 disk STOP). Never start two at once. `verify.sh` deletes its own logs at the start of a run, which is why the scan copies them.

**Facts from P5.7a (2026-10-21)**
- Pieces: `Editor/BookEditorModel` (no Avalonia: the fields of Summary and Details as properties, `Load`/`ApplyTo`/`Save`/`Apply`/`Ok`/`GoPrevious`/`GoNext`/`LinkFile`/`RunScript`, the enable and visibility rules of `SetComicToEditor`, the language list, the prompts of the proposed values, `ThreeD`, `CustomThumbnailKey`, `Values(EditorList)`; P5.7b and P5.7c add their fields next to these in `Load` and `ApplyTo`), `Editor/SpinRules`, `Editor/ListSelectorModel`, `Editor/BookTileText` (`ComicTextBuilder` as style data: the stand-in has no `Font`), `Editor/Cover3D`, Host `Library/EditorValueLists` (port of `DefaultLists`: plain lists, a `"-"` entry marks a separator, `WithEmpty` and `Completions` are what the combo boxes and the text boxes collect) and `Library/IconPackages`; chrome: `CrSpinButton`, `CrBookTile` (+ `BookTileLayout`, `TileTextLine`, `ThumbnailShadow` extracted from `CrThumbnailItem`), `CrValueComboBox` (+ `ValueItem`, `PackageIcons`), `CrCheckList`; views: `ComicBookDialog` (frame `CrDialogWindow` with the new `ButtonBarMargin`), `InfoSummaryPage`, `InfoDetailsPage` (XAML generated from the Designer's coordinates: a margin is a position, the alignment is the anchor), `ListSelectorView`, `DesignerLayout.Place`; `MainViewModel.BookInfo.cs`; `ILibraryDialogs.ShowBookInfoAsync(BookInfoRequest)` (default: cancel; `FakeDialogs.BookInfos` and `BookInfoHandler` in tests). Registered view: `ComicBookDialog` (`--show ComicBookDialog` opens the dialog of the first book without blocking).
- The dialog edits the library's own book (`current`): nothing is written while fields change, OK, Apply, Previous and Next write (`SaveBook`), Cancel and Escape write nothing; Apply and every move call `RefreshInfoFromFile` and the browser's `SelectBook` again, as `SetCurrentBook` does. `MainViewModel.ShowInfoAsync` sets the undo marker "Show Info" first (so one Undo takes back everything the dialog wrote, also across Previous and Next) and logs `Info dialog opened for <caption> (N books in the list).` and `Info dialog closed with OK|Cancel.` (the e2e case reads them). Several selected books that can all be edited will open the multi edit dialog (P5.8): until then the status bar says so and nothing opens. The page menu of the reader opens the dialog for the book of the tab alone (PC.01 note).
- Undo and Redo: the Core has no change event for the undo list (`ComicBookContainerUndo` only subscribes `BookChanged`, so book inserts and removals are not steps either). `MainViewModel` subscribes `Database.BookChanged` while a host is open and calls `RefreshUndoState()` on the UI thread (`CanUndo`, `CanRedo`, `UndoText`, `RedoText`, `UndoToolTip`, `RedoToolTip`, `UndoCommand`, `RedoCommand`); the Edit menu items (Cmd+Z, Cmd+Y, alias Cmd+Shift+Z) and the toolbar buttons (`CrToolButton` dims by `IsEffectivelyEnabled`: a disabled command leaves `IsEnabled` true) follow it. While the Info dialog is open (`IsInfoDialogOpen`) Undo, Redo and Info... are off so that Cmd+Z and Cmd+Y reach the text field (a native menu item takes its key before the focused control).
- Icon packages: the repository holds `Resources/Icons/{Publishers,AgeRatings*,Formats,Special}` as plain folders (the Windows build zips them); `IconPackages.Load` reads a folder or a zip per package once per process (`VirtualFileFolder`, `ZipFileFolder`), called by `MacHost.ConfigureCore` for `<program>/Resources/Icons` and `<data>/Resources/Icons`, with the key rules of `Program.SplitIconKeys` and `SplitIconKeysWithYearAndMonth`. `ComicBook.GetIcons()` therefore returns pictures now (8,381 publisher keys). `PackageIcons.Get(IconSet, key)` makes the Avalonia image once per picture.
- 3D cover: `Cover3D.Render(SKImage cover, int pages)` runs the Core's `ComicBox3D.CreateDefaultBook` on the stand-in (50 to 200 ms). The default options (0x17) include `Filter` (render at 1.5 times the size, scale back by two thirds), which fails here: `GraphicsExtensions.HighQuality` calls `Graphics.TransformPoints`, which the stand-in does not have, so `ImageProcessing.ResizeGdi(highQuality: true)` returns null (`BitmapExtensions.Resize` swallows the exception) and `ComicBox3D` returns null with `Filter`. `Cover3D` therefore asks for 768 x 768 without `Filter` and scales the trimmed result with Skia (the same picture). Thumbnails come from `ThumbnailService.RequestResult(book, FrontCover, 512, ...)`, which honours `CustomThumbnailKey`.
- Layout: the tab pages are 566 x 442 as the Designer's when `TabControl.Padding` is (3, 0, 3, 3) (the stock control leaves 572 x 445; the headless font makes the pages 440 high, tests allow 438 to 446 and compare bottom anchored controls with the page height). Autosize labels have no width (the text of macOS is wider than the Designer's autosized width); a right anchored one has 60 units of slack to its left; `TextBox` and `AutoCompleteBox` need `MinWidth="0"` (the stock minimum is 64) and the 16 wide selector buttons `MinWidth="0" MinHeight="0"`; `SplitButton` `MinHeight="23"`. A `ControlTheme` of a control that derives from a stock control must not override `StyleKeyOverride`: its setters apply only when the theme's `TargetType` equals the style key (found with `CrValueComboBox`: the item template of its theme was ignored until the override was removed). `ComicBookDialog` keeps the last tab in a static (`lastActivePage`), so a test selects its tab itself.
- Quirks of the original that are kept: `TextBoxEx` sets its text to the prompt when it gets the focus and never clears it again (its `hasEdited` flag is set by that very assignment); the Day spin button starts at the current month (`AddUpDown(txDay, DateTime.Now.Month, 1, 31)`); `EditControlUtility.GetNumber` turns a negative or unparsable number into -1; the Catalog tab is hidden for a book with a file by default (`CatalogOnlyForFileless` is true) and the Custom tab by default (`ShowCustomBookFields` is false).
- Settings (Mac only): `InfoDialogWidth` and `InfoDialogHeight` (0 = the minimum size; OK and Apply save a size above it), `RecentThumbnailFiles` (`AddRecentThumbnailFile`, 20 files). `InformationCover3D`, `UpdateComicFiles`, `UpdateComicBookFiles`, `CatalogOnlyForFileless`, `ShowCustomBookFields` were mirrored by P5.1 and are read by the dialog.
- Tests: `Editor/BookEditorModelTests` (16: fields, trimming, -1 numbers, OK, Apply, Cancel, Previous and Next, enable rules, prompts, languages, value lists and `DefaultLists.txt`, custom thumbnail, Link to File, 3D setting, one undo step across two books), `Editor/InfoDialogTests` (25: tabs, positions, texts, edit flows, spin buttons, prompts, lists, list selector, cover, wheel, thumbnail menu, link, last tab, Escape), `Editor/InfoCommandTests` (7: Info enabled by the selection, the dialog's books, multi selection, one Undo and Redo with labels, Cmd+Z off while the dialog is open, toolbar and Edit menu follow the undo list, the page menu, the view registry), `Editor/BookTileTests` (19, three at 1x and 2x: tile geometry, text lines, 3D render, icon packages, combo box values, spin buttons, flat and 3D tile frames). The existing tests that named Edit, the page menu, the Tools menu and the toolbar were updated. e2e case `info-edit` passed on the unlocked screen: Cmd+I opens the dialog (593 x 545 points with the title bar), Series edited on Details, OK, the grid and the next dialog show the new caption, `Undo: Show Info` in the Edit menu, the saved library has the series, Escape writes nothing after a relaunch. Contact sheet: `mac/artifacts/chrome/P5.7a/sheet.png` and `sheet.html` (ComicBookDialog light and dark from a real window). View spec `ComicBookDialog.md`. Chrome decisions added: C-DLG-5, C-CTRL-3, C-INFO-1.


**Facts from P5.7b (2026-10-21)**
- Pages: `Views/InfoPlotPage` (tabPlot: the sub-tabs Summary, Notes, Review in `NotesTabs`, Characters, Teams, Locations, Main Character or Team, Scan Information, Web), `Views/InfoCatalogPage` (tabCatalog), `Views/InfoCustomPage` (tabCustom); each `Initialize(model)`, XAML generated from the Designer's coordinates as for the Details page (a margin is a position, the alignment is the anchor; a right anchored autosize label is 60 wider than the Designer's width; a box with a list is 16 narrower, the selector button beside it; the Pages box is 11 narrower, its spin button is hidden but takes the room). `ComicBookDialog.PlotPage`, `CatalogPage`, `CustomPage` are these pages (they were empty panels), `ShowTab(key)` selects a tab (`COMICRACK_MAC_INFO_TAB=tabPlot` makes `--show ComicBookDialog` open on that tab; the contact sheet uses it). `Views/ListSelectorFlyout.Open` is the one place that opens the list selector (Details uses it too).
- Model (`BookEditorModel`): new fields `Summary`, `Notes`, `Review`, `Characters`, `Teams`, `MainCharacterOrTeam`, `Locations`, `ScanInformation`, `Web`, `BookStore`, `BookPrice` (text), `BookAge`, `BookCondition`, `BookOwner`, `BookLocation`, `BookCollectionStatus`, `BookNotes`, `Isbn`, `PagesAsTextSimple`, `AddedTime`, `ReleasedTime`, `OpenedTime`, `ShowFilelessCatalogFields`, `CustomRows`; loaded in `Load`, written in `ApplyTo` in the order of `SaveBook`. The multi-line texts are loaded with `StringUtility.MakeEditBoxMultiline` (CR LF) and the boxes use `NewLine` = CR LF: a library saved on a Mac and on Windows holds the same text, and `ComicInfo.CheckMultilineEquality` makes an untouched text no change. `Web` is the editable box; `WebLink` (the link under the Summary tab) still shows the address the book had when it was shown, as `linkLabel`.
- Dates: `BookEditorModel.PickerValue` is the range of a `DateTimePicker` (1753-01-01 to 9998-12-31; outside it "not set" = `DateTime.MinValue`, saved as such). `CalendarDatePicker` raises `SelectedDateChanged` a moment after the value is set, so a test settles the window after setting a date (`Dpi.Settle`); `InfoCatalogPage.Pick` keeps the time of day of the old value; the box does not echo a day it already shows. Text changes of the Custom grid's cells are read from the `Text` property, not from `TextChanged` (which comes later too).
- Custom tab: `CustomValueRow` (name, value, `Hidden` for a script key), `BookEditorModel.CustomRows` (the keys of `EditorValueLists.GetCustomValueKeys()` = `ComicLibrary.GetCustomFields(books, showAll: true)`, read once per dialog and extended by what the dialog saves and by the shown book's own keys, ordered with the default string comparer as `OrderBy(s => s)`; then one empty row; `EnsureNewCustomRow()` adds the next one), `CustomValueSuggestions(key)` (`EditorValueLists.GetCustomValues`, the completions of the value cell), `ShowScriptValues` (`ExtendedSettings.ShowCustomScriptValues`, false; the Mac has no extended settings file). `CustomValuesStore()` builds the store from every row with a name and a value (the name is not trimmed, as in the original) and the book gets it as one assignment. The grid has no row headers; there is no DataGrid package, so the grid is composed (`CrHeader` + `GridSplitter` over rows of `TextBox` and `AutoCompleteBox`; classes `cr-grid`, `cr-grid-line`, `cr-grid-cell`). The column header texts are looked up under `CustomValueName` and `CustomValueValue` as the Designer names the columns; the language files have `CustomValueKey` for the first, so only "Value" is translated, as in the original.
- Visibility and state follow the model flags from P5.7a: the Catalog tab for a book without a file or with `CatalogOnlyForFileless` off, the Custom tab with `ShowCustomBookFields`, both only when the book is in a container or `UpdateComicBookFiles` is on; Opened/Read and Pages for a book without a file (`ShowFilelessCatalogFields`, only such a book writes `PagesAsTextSimple` and `OpenedTime`: the check is on the target book after its `FilePath` was set, as `SaveBook` does); Scan Information with `ShowProposedValues` (a book with a file). `lastActiveTextPage` keeps the sub-tab. A price is a combo box of text: `BookPriceAsText` (current culture) is compared and `GetRealNumber` (current culture, so "7.25" is 725 on a system with the comma as decimal separator, as in the original) is written.
- Tests: `Editor/BookEditorModelTextTabsTests` (16: the fields, line endings, trimming, multi-line equality, moving between books, price, dates and the picker range, tab flags, value lists, custom rows, script values, saving rows, suggestions) and `Editor/InfoTextTabsTests` (24: positions of all three tabs at the Designer's coordinates and with a wider window, labels, sub-tabs and their memory, edit flows and OK or Cancel, Characters without line breaks, selectors, lists, date boxes, Pages digits, visibility, the grid, a new row, script values, suggestions, the frames). No custom drawn control or draw operation was added (`CrHeader` and stock controls with classes), so there is no new HiDpi test. Frames: `mac/artifacts/chrome/P5.7b/sheet.png` (ComicBookDialog on the Plot & Notes tab, light and dark, from a real window with `COMICRACK_MAC_INFO_TAB=tabPlot`); the Catalog and Custom tabs are checked in headless frames (`CRMAC_FRAMES_DIR`, `info-tabCatalog@1x.png`, `info-tabCustom@1x.png`). Chrome decision added: C-INFO-2. View spec `ComicBookDialog.md` extended.

**Facts from P5.7c (2026-10-21)**
- Pieces: Editor `PageListModel` (the rows, `PageColumns`, sorting, selection, every command of the page menu, `SavePagesTo`; no Avalonia, also the model of the pages view of P5.23b; `PageRow`, `PageTileText`), `PageRowThumbnails` (the pictures, kept per image index, rotation and step), `ColorTabModel` (the five sliders in percent, the white point, the page buttons), `ColorPreview` (page scaled to the viewer's width in device pixels and the Core's colour code applied off the UI thread, one at a time, the newest request wins; a replaced page bitmap is not disposed because an adjustment may still read it), `IPageImageSource` and `CorePageImageSource` (the page from the pool through `GetPage(key, book)`, copied under `ItemMonitor`); `BookEditorModel` gets `ColorTab` (not `Colors`: `NoLiteralColorsTests` reads `Colors.X` as a colour literal), `PageServices`, `Pages`, `EnsurePages()`, `Close()`, `PagesChanged`, `ThumbnailsChanged` and `ThumbnailBooks`. Controls `CrPageList` (a `Control` that draws itself; two stock `ScrollBar`s are its visual and logical children; rows through `IPageListSource`, `PageListColumn`, `PageRowState`, `PageListMode`), `CrPageViewer`, and three shared pieces taken out of existing controls: `CrHeader.Draw` (static), `TileTextPainter` (from `CrBookTile`), `ThumbnailAdornments` (the page number badge and the bookmark flag, from `CrThumbnailItem`). Views `InfoPagesPage`, `InfoColorsPage`, `PagesContextMenu`, `SelectItemWindow`. `ThumbnailService.Invalidate(book)`, `BrowserViewModel.RefreshCovers()`. View specs `PagesView.md`, `SelectItemDialog.md`, `ComicBookDialog.md` extended; decision C-INFO-3.
- Pages work on a copy (`new ComicBook(book)`) with a navigator of its own, made by `EnsurePages()` when the tab is first shown for a book (the original makes it for every book; here moving through books opens no file, and the copy and its navigator go when another book is shown, the dialog closes or Apply reloads the book). Nothing reaches the library's book until Save: `SavePagesTo` does what `SaveBook` does (`SetPages`, `PageCount` of the file, `LastPageRead` kept inside it) and only once the navigator's index is complete.
- Quirks of the Core that the model meets: the navigator teaches its book the file sizes of the pages as the index arrives (`UpdatePageFileSize`), the thumbnail work teaches the sizes (`CacheManager.UpdateComicBookPageData` for the key's source, the copy), so Size, Width and Height fill in while the tab is open and a first save always differs from the book by them (the cover key does not change: `SavePagesTo(book, out coverChanged)`); `ComicPageInfo.Equals` ignores the rotation, so `SetPages` does nothing for a change of rotations alone, which the original loses, and `SavePagesTo` applies the rotations page by page; a front cover page that the navigator finds with a name that is not a valid cover key becomes "Other" (`ProviderImageIndexReady`); `ComicInfo.UpdatePageType(cpi, ...)` and `MovePages` need the pages in `Pages` (the navigator's file size updates add them); `ImageRotation.None` is translated "None", not "0°" (the `ImageRotation` section).
- The item view's rules that were read and kept: Detail is the default view, the sort starts on the Page column, a header click or Arrange by item sorts by the column and a second one flips, rows are the item font plus 6 high (12 to 48), thumbnails 128 (96 to 512), tiles 192 x 96 (height 64 to 512), Cmd and the wheel resize (one notch is 1 for rows and 16 for the others); a selection is by `UpdateSelectionFromMouse` (the anchor is the page of the last plain click; Cmd+click and Shift+click leave it); the thumbnail column asks for 256 and is as wide as its cell, so a row cuts the picture off (the original does); the view, sizes, column widths and sort are static fields of `InfoPagesPage` (`ComicBookDialog.PagesConfig`) and `InfoPagesPage.ResetViewState()` and `InfoColorsPage.ResetViewState()` reset them for each test (`TestGuard`).
- The menu: the enum menus are built as `EnumMenuUtility` does (the browsable values, the translated names, Alt+Shift+A for the types and Alt+Shift+6 for the rotations; DD20 leaves Alt and Shift as they are), the bookmark and move shortcuts are Cmd and Cmd+Option; `PagesContextMenu.TryRun(key, modifiers)` is how a key reaches an item (a flyout item has no key while it is closed); Backspace is an alias of Delete (the Mac's Delete key); Copy Page is present and off. The enable and visibility rules are those of `contextPages_Opening` and the `CommandMapper` of `OnLoad`.
- Colors: the sliders show `(int)(value * 100)` of the book's adjustment (a value of 0.337 shows 33 and is written as 0.33, as in the original), `HasGamma` goes through `CreateAdjustedBitmap` too (`BitmapAdjustment.IsEmpty` does not look at the gamma), the white point is picked from the page as it was read (`CreateColorWhitePointMatrix` neutralises the colour, it does not make it exactly white), a double click is `CrPageViewer.Picked` with the place as fractions of the picture, the page is read when the tab is shown and again when the viewer is more than 24 device pixels wider or narrower. The stock `Slider` has no ticks (C-INFO-3).
- After the dialog `MainViewModel.ShowInfoAsync` invalidates the thumbnails of the books whose cover key changed (another front cover page, a turned cover, a custom thumbnail) and calls `Browser.RefreshCovers()` (every cell asks again; `BrowserViewModel.Thumbnails` is raised). `ThumbnailService.Invalidate(book)` drops every cached picture of a book (all pages, sizes and remembered failures) and makes a running decode deliver to its callers without caching; the Core's own pool is dropped by `PageListModel.RefreshSelected` (`Pages.RefreshImage`, `Thumbs.RefreshImage`).
- Tests: `Editor/PageListModelTests` (17 on a real CBZ: rows, columns, sorting, the menu's commands, order commands, Save, pictures, Merge Pages), `Editor/PageListControlTests` (11: geometry, scroll bars, selection by mouse and keys, headers and widths, sizes, the three views, pictures only for visible rows), `Editor/ColorTabModelTests` (11: percent, Reset, buttons, the preview with the Core's colour code, overtaking requests), `Editor/InfoPagesColorsTests` (23: positions at the Designer's coordinates, texts, the menu and its keys, OK and Cancel, Previous and Next, the Colors tab end to end), `Editor/SelectItemWindowTests` (4: the name dialog and the bookmark that asks for it), `HiDpi/PageListHiDpiTests` (6 at 1x and 2x), 4 `ThumbnailServiceTests` for `Invalidate`, `InfoCommandTests` (page services and cover refresh). e2e `info-pages` (Pages tab lists the 24 pages of a fixture book, Delete marks the third page, the Colors tab opens its sliders, OK writes the page type into the saved library; the Pages tab is at (216, 48) and the Colors tab at (262, 48) while Catalog and Custom are hidden). Contact sheets `mac/artifacts/chrome/P5.7c/sheet.png` (ComicBookDialog on the Pages tab, light and dark, from a real window) and `mac/artifacts/chrome/P5.7c-colors/sheet.png` (Colors tab). Chrome decision added: C-INFO-3.


**Facts from P5.8 (2026-10-21)**
- Pieces: Editor `MultiBookEditorModel` (no Avalonia: `MultiField` per box with `Text`, `Date`, `Check` (`MultiCheck`: Unchecked, Checked, Indeterminate), `Enabled`, `Visible`, `PromptText`; `Store()`, `GetSameValue`, `SpinFor`, `RatingOf`/`RatingText`, `Values(EditorList)`, `Languages`, `CustomFields`, the group flags `ShowCatalog`, `ShowCustom`, `AnyLinked`, `AllInContainer`), `EditorListValues.Get(lists, list, rawTitle)` (the value lists moved out of `BookEditorModel.Values`; the multi dialog's Title list is the raw `Title`, the Info dialog's the shadow title), `BookEditorModel.BuildLanguages` is internal now; views `MultipleComicBooksDialog` (frame `CrDialogWindow`, 525 x 460, OK 77 wide), `MultipleBooksBody` (axaml generated from the Designer's coordinates, 56 boxes, each with `chk<Box>`, optional `<Box>Selector` and `<Box>Spin`; the custom fields built in code), `MultiEditViewState` (collapsed groups and scroll position, kept for the run: `Reset()` is in `TestGuard`); `Services/MultipleBooksRequest`, `ILibraryDialogs.ShowMultipleBooksAsync` (default cancels; `FakeDialogs.MultipleBooks` and `MultipleBooksHandler`); `MainViewModel.ShowMultipleBooksAsync(books)` and `CreateMultipleBooksRequest`; `AppPreferences.TabLayouts` (mirrored, enum `ComicRack.Mac.Host.TabLayouts` copies the original's flags); chrome: class `cr-framed-panel` (`CrParts.axaml`), `CrCollapsibleGroup` is used by a view for the first time. Registered view: `MultipleComicBooksDialog` (`--show MultipleComicBooksDialog`: the first 12 books of the library, `COMICRACK_MAC_MULTI_BOOKS` sets the number; it reads the books as they are and writes only on OK).
- Rules read from the original and kept: every box shows the common value or nothing; a box whose books agree is **checked**, so OK writes it again with the same value (51 of the 56 boxes of the fixture library are checked at the start); typing checks, a click toggles (indeterminate becomes unchecked and empties a list box); a list box whose books differ is indeterminate and OK replaces the entries that every book had (the intersection) by the box's entries and keeps each book's other entries, in the order of a `HashSet` (a removed and re-added entry takes the freed slot, so "Stan Lee, Jack Kirby" becomes "Stan Lee, Jack Kirby, Roy Thomas" and not the other way round: the original does the same); a disabled box is never written; `EnableProposed` is a bool (any text but "Yes" is false); a number that does not parse or is negative is the property's `DefaultValue` (-1); `Rating` text is `float.ToString()` of the current culture; `MakeCheckBox` looks for the label with `base.Controls[...]`, which only searches the form's direct children, so the labels never move (the check box and the box shift, the label stays at the Designer's x).
- Layout facts: the group is measured from its top including the 24 high header, so a control's Designer y minus 24 is its position in the content panel; the check box is 16 x 17 (an auto-sized empty `CheckBox`), vertically `(h - 17) / 2`; the number boxes' spin button sits at `x + w - 10` after `AddUpDown` made the box 11 narrower, and the check box then moves only the box; the selector button is at `x + w - 16`. The custom group's height is `lastBoxBottom + 32` and its panel `lastBoxBottom + 8` (one unit frame): label 12 high at y 8, box at y 23, the next label 8 below the last box. The pointer double click toggles tab mode only when the press lands on the group's panel itself or the custom canvas (a label or a box keeps its own double click): `DoubleTapped` bubbles in Avalonia, so the handler compares `e.Source`.
- Tab mode (`RegisterPanelToTabToggle`): the group panels are moved into `ScrollViewer`s that are the pages of a `TabControl` with the 3, 0, 3, 3 padding (page titles = group titles, a hidden group has no page); the panels are the same controls in both modes, so the double tap handler is attached once and the boxes keep their bindings. The flag is `TabLayouts.Multiple` of the saved setting.
- `CrCollapsibleGroup.Render` skips the header text while the control has no width: a hidden group (Custom Fields) is rendered once with width 0 and `FormattedText.MaxTextHeight` refuses a text height of zero (same crash class as `CrTextOverlay` in P5.32a).
- The refresh of the books' information (`RefreshInfoFromFile(None)`) runs on the pool before the dialog opens, without a progress dialog (DL.17 is P5.26); the log has `Multi edit: refreshing the information of N books.`, `Multi edit dialog opened for N books.` and `Multi edit dialog closed with OK|Cancel.` (the e2e case reads the last two). `ShowInfoAsync` calls it for several editable books and sets the undo marker "Edit multiple Books" (`UndoEditMultipleComics`) inside `ShowMultipleBooksAsync`.
- Tests: `Editor/MultiBookEditorModelTests` (23: shown values, checked and unchecked boxes, Yes/No texts, numbers and dates, the 56 properties, typing and toggling, enable rules, prompts, list boxes and the replace rule, what `Store` converts, a disabled box, progress, spin settings, ratings, custom fields, value lists), `Editor/MultiEditDialogTests` (23: window and button positions, group heights, every box and its check box, selector and spin button against the Designer file, label positions and texts, check boxes (typing, toggle, indeterminate, a real click), disabled boxes and hidden catalog fields, drop-down lists, rating stars by a real press, number keys and spin buttons, the selector flyout, the prompt on focus, OK, Cancel, Escape, the modal dialog's result, collapsed groups and scroll position kept, tab mode by a real double click and kept in the setting, custom fields, the frame line and header pixels in light and dark at 1x and 2x), `InfoCommandTests` (two: several books open the multi dialog and one Undo takes back what OK wrote in all of them with the labels "Undo: Edit multiple Books", Cancel writes nothing), `SettingsStoreTests` (`TabLayouts`). e2e case `multi-edit` (Cmd+A, Cmd+I: the 525 x 488 window, typing in the Series box, OK writes the series into all 50 books (the saved library has it 50 times), "Undo: Edit multiple Books", a second run: Escape writes nothing) passed on the unlocked screen. Contact sheet: `mac/artifacts/chrome/P5.8/sheet.png` and `sheet.html` (MultipleComicBooksDialog over 12 fixture books, light and dark, from a real window). View spec `MultipleComicBooksDialog.md`. Chrome decision added: C-DLG-6. Ledger: DL.02 done, DL.02a added (todo: Paste, Preferences and Export take the tab toggle).

**Facts from P5.9 (2026-10-23)**
- Host `Library/BookFileUpdates` (no UI) is `MainForm`'s file update logic around the Core's `QueueManager.AddBookToFileUpdate`: `Start()` subscribes `Database.BookChanged` and `BookFactory.TemporaryBookChanged` (`WatchedBookHasChanged`: a local book with `FileInfoRetrieved` gets `ComicInfoIsDirty` or `ComicBookIsDirty` set whatever the settings say, and is queued unless a reader has it open), `BookClosed(book)` queues a book when its tab closes, `QueueDirtyBooks()` is `OnLoad`'s offer of every book, `UpdateBooks(books)` (books with a file, `alwaysWrite`), `UpdateAll()` (library plus temporary books), `IsWriting`, `PendingCount`, `Errors` (file name and message, the list for the Errors dialog of P5.26), `ErrorCount`. `MainViewModel.FileUpdates` is made per host in `OnHostOpened` (log line `File updates ready: N books looked at. Writing on|off, automatic on|off.`) and disposed when the host closes.
- The Core queue ignores every offer while `UpdateComicFiles` ("Allow writing of Book info into files", default off) is off, and without `alwaysWrite` also while `AutoUpdateComicsFiles` is off; so with the defaults an edit only marks the book (the thumbnail's update badge) until Update Book Files or Update all Book Files writes it. There is no Preferences dialog yet (P5.22a); tests and the e2e case set the values in `AppPreferences` / `Settings.xml`. The commands log `Update all Book Files: N books offered to the write queue.` and add a note when writing is off.
- A failed 7zz run raises the provider's error event more than once for one book (three entries for one failure), so `UpdateErrors` can list the same book several times; the original does the same.
- The Core marks books dirty by itself as soon as it learns something new from the file (page sizes from the cover decode), so after Update all Book Files unedited books may be rewritten too (the e2e case reports it as info).
- UI: File > Update all Book Files (Cmd+Shift+U, `UpdateAllItem`, hidden while the automatic update is on), context menu item `miUpdateComicFiles` (`BookCommand.UpdateFiles`), status bar slots `WriteInfoSlot` (`IsWriteInfoActive`, tool tip `tsWriteInfoActivity.Tooltip`) and `UpdateErrorsSlot` (`HasUpdateErrors`, tool tip as `UpdateActivityTimerTick` builds it) between the book segment and the page segments; `MainWindow` refreshes them with a 1000 ms `DispatcherTimer` (`MainViewModel.RefreshActivity`). The pictures are still (`UpdateInfoAnimation`, `ExportError`); animation and the click actions are P5.26.
- Tests: `BookFileUpdatesTests` (9, Host.Tests: real CBZ books and the bundled 7zz; marking, automatic write, open book waits for its tab, Update all, writing off, the start offer, no file, a failed write in a read-only folder, dispose), `Editor/FileUpdateTests` (5, Mac.Tests: Info dialog edit then context menu item, `crcli info show` after quit reads the values; menu item shortcut and hiding; automatic update and tab close; status bar icons follow the queue; no library), `MainWindowHiDpiTests` (slots at 1x and 2x), `ContextMenuTests` updated. They return quietly when `mac/native/fetch-7zz.sh` was never run. e2e case `write-back` passed on the unlocked screen (edit Series in the Info dialog, file untouched, Cmd+Shift+U, `crcli info show` reads the new Series); `menu-structure` gained the Update all Book Files item; its later checks fail intermittently with `Can't get menu bar item` when another app takes the focus (environment, SKIPPED(focus)); the File items passed in two runs.

**Facts from P4.4 (2026-10-27)**
- `PluginPackages(ScriptFolders, ScriptHost?)` in `mac/ComicRack.Mac.Host/Scripting/PluginPackages.cs` wraps the original `PackageManager` (packages in `<data>/Scripts/<Name>`, pending in `<data>/Scripts/.Pending`): `Install` (stage), `InstallNow`, `Commit` (the app calls it once at start, before `ScriptHost.Start`), `Uninstall(name)`, `RemovePending`, `Exists(file)` (the "replace?" question), `List()`, `CommandsOf(name)`, `SetEnabled(name, bool)`. It does the install step of `Commit` itself with full paths (the original deletes with bare names, see `mac/upstream-proposals/P4.4-packagemanager-safedelete.md`) and refuses package names that are not plain folder names. Enable/disable switches the commands whose script file lies in the package folder; the state reaches `Preferences.PluginsStates` through `ScriptHost.StoreCommandStates()` (preferences are not saved to disk yet: P5.28b). crcli: `script install <file> [--stage]`, `script list`, `script remove <name> [--stage]` (all on `--data-dir`).

**Facts from P4.3 (2026-10-27)**
- Scripting host: `mac/ComicRack.Mac.Host/Scripting/` has `ScriptHost` (one per `MacHost`, `host.Scripts`; `ScriptUtility.Initialize` of the original: reads the script folders, applies `PluginsStates`, sets `ComicBookPluginMatcher.PluginEngine`, wires `ParseComicPath` into `ComicBook.ParseFilePath`, makes a `ScriptSearch` per `NetSearch` command; `GetCommands`, `Invoke`, `RunStartup`, `RunShutdown(userClosing)`, `HasListCommand`, `LoadCommands(file)`, `AttachConsole`, `StoreCommandStates`; `Dispose` takes the static hooks out), `MacPluginEnvironment` (own `IPluginEnvironment`, the object a script sees as `ComicRack`; window parts come from the current `IScriptUi`), `MacScriptApplication` (+ `MacScriptBrowser`, `MacScriptOpenBooks`), `IScriptUi`/`HeadlessScriptUi` (headless answers: AskQuestion 0, new book dialog cancelled, nothing open, every window request recorded in `Requests`), `ScriptFolders` (bundled `Scripts/` next to the executable, `<data>/Scripts`, extra), `ScriptRunner` (what `crcli script run` does), `TextWriterStream`. `MacHostOptions.Scripting` (null = the new preference `Scripting`, default on, mirrors Settings.cs), `.ScriptFolders`, `.ScriptUi`. New mirrored preferences `Scripting`, `ScriptingLibraries`, `PluginsStates`.
- The app does not use it yet (P4.5): it must set `host.Scripts.Ui` once windows exist, call `RunStartup()` after the main window shows and `RunShutdown(userClosing)` when it closes (a `false` answer cancels a user close), call `StoreCommandStates()` when settings are saved, install the Dispatcher `UnhandledException` handler (Facts from P4.2) and switch `Theme.Dark` with the appearance.
- IronPython patch: `mac/tools/patch-ironpython` (Mono.Cecil; `FILE`, `IN OUT`, `--check FILE`; idempotent). `mac/Directory.Build.targets` runs it after the build on every project whose output holds `IronPython.dll` (app, crcli, test projects; not spikes); the tool is built first through a project reference of `ComicRack.Plugins.Core` (`AppendRuntimeIdentifierToOutputPath=false` in the tool keeps a `-r osx-arm64` publish from moving its output). `make-app.sh` patches the bundle copy, checks `Contents/MacOS/Lib/os.py`, copies the IronPython licence to `Contents/Resources/IronPython-License.txt`; `IronPython.dll` is excluded from ReadyToRun (a native image cannot be patched). `IronPython.StdLib` 2.7.12 is a package reference of the Host project; `Lib/` flows to every executable.
- A script command key is the method name; `PythonPluginInitializer` only sees functions whose names have two or more characters. `IsHook` is a substring test; one command with several hooks (`BookOpened, ReaderResized`) gets the delegate type of the last hook that matches, so write one function per hook. A matcher with `MatchOperator` 1 and no `PluginKey` returns all books (original behaviour): it is treated as needing no script when scripting is on.
- Lists: `LibraryLists.BuildTree(db, scripts)`, `ListEvaluator(useStoredCaches, scripts)`, `RequiresScripts(item, scripts)`, `MissingScripts(item, scripts)`; `ListNode.ScriptsDisabled` and `.MissingScripts`. The sidebar badge is "Requires scripts" (scripting off) or "Script not found: key" (on, script missing); an evaluator without scripts behaves as before. crcli: `script run`, `script expr`, and `smartlist` take `--no-scripts` and `--scripts-dir`; crcli and the test projects get the bundled `Scripts/` like the app.
- The fixture library's two script lists name the keys `findMissingIssues` (Missing (to fix)) and `NextIssuesToRead` (Next issues to read); the scripts that provide them are the owner's and are not in the repository. With the test stand-ins `mac/tests/ComicRack.Mac.Host.Tests/Scripting/TestScripts/ListScripts.py` (same keys, simple bodies: the book after a gap in a series; the first unread book of each series) `crcli smartlist mac/fixtures/ComicDb.xml --all --remap ... --scripts-dir <TestScripts>` gives Missing (to fix) 2 and Next issues to read 4312 (about 3.5 s for the whole library); without the scripts both show "script not found: <key>" and 0. The real numbers need the owner's scripts (OA-P43 in section 9).
- Acceptance headless hooks run from a rewritten temp copy of `ComicRack/Output/Scripts` (the reference lines of System.Windows.Forms and System.Drawing name the stand-in assemblies): Sample.py ParseComicPath (Sample.xml), CreateBookList, NetSearch, ComicInfoHtml, QuickOpenHtml; CommitProposed.py (asks through `ComicRack.App.AskQuestion` and `ComicRack.Localize`), SetComicInfoDirty. Bundle: `make-app.sh` patched the copy, `Contents/MacOS/Lib` and the licence are there, and `COMICRACK_ENABLE_SELFTEST=1 ComicRack.Mac --script-selftest mac/spikes/S4-ironpython/selftest.py` with `COMICRACK_SELFTEST_REWRITE_REFS=1` printed 22 PASS and no FAIL (Sample.py hooks, `class UIControl(Control)`). Tests: Host.Tests `Scripting/` (IronPythonPatchTests 4, ScriptHostTests 14, ScriptListTests 5, BundledSampleTests 6, ScriptApplicationTests 5), Cli.Tests `ScriptCommandTests` (5).
- `IApplication.ReadDatabaseBooks` throws in the original `MainForm`; here it returns the books (as `PluginEnvironment` does). `GetBook(file)` is `Create(file, AddToTemporary)` without reading the info (call `RefreshInfoFromFile()`), as in the original. `ProductVersion` is the constant "0.9.184" (a test compares it with `ComicRack/Properties/AssemblyInfo.cs`).


**Facts from P4.6a (2026-10-27)**
- `crcli script-scan <file-or-folder>... [--json F] [--top N] [--reference-pack DIR] [--fail-on-error]` (`mac/ComicRack.Cli/Scripting/ScriptScanCommand.cs`) over the engine in `mac/ComicRack.Mac.Host/Scripting/Scan/` (namespace `ComicRack.Mac.Host.Scripting.Scan`; usable by the app for the per-script report of P4.8/P4.12 and at install time): `ScriptScanner.Scan(paths)` gives a `ScanReport` (`Units`, `Types`, `Members`, `References`, `UntypedMembers`, `Tiers`, `ToJson()`, `WriteText`), `ScanPython(text)` and `ScanAssembly(bytes)` give one `ScanUnit` (for a report at install time). It reads `.py` (IronPython parser, Python 2 syntax), `.dll`/`.exe` (System.Reflection.Metadata), `.html` and `.crplugin`/`.zip` (in memory, never unpacked); nothing is run or loaded. Grouping: a unit's package is the plugin package file, the first sub folder, or the file itself; `scripts` in the tables = units, `packages` = plugins. 516 files of the Python 2.7 library parse in 0.7 s.
- The catalog (`ApiCatalog`) is the real surface read from metadata: the reference assemblies of the Windows Desktop pack (`--reference-pack`, env `COMICRACK_WINFORMS_REFERENCE`, or the NuGet cache `microsoft.windowsdesktop.app.ref`, which a pass A build downloads; 1111 types of `System.Windows.Forms*` and `System.Drawing*`, `System.Drawing.Primitives` from the runtime). Without the pack it falls back to the facade assemblies next to the program and says `(incomplete)` in the report and in the JSON (`catalog.complete`); after P4.7 that fallback becomes the full surface. Tests that need the pack throw with the pass A hint when it is missing (as the Compat tests do).
- Name resolution: star and explicit imports, `import a.b as c`, attribute chains, script classes deriving from a surface type (inherited members counted, the script's own methods and attributes not; overridden virtual members counted), variable, `self` attribute, property and method result types as far as the catalog tells (two passes), parameters of a `DrawThumbnailOverlay` hook (the plugin contract: `Graphics`, `Rectangle`). Members are reported against the type that declares them (`ButtonBase.Text`, `Control+ControlCollection::Add`; display `Control.ControlCollection.Add`), constructors as `T..ctor`; compiled accessors of assemblies (`set_Text`) map to the property. Accesses on values of unknown type are counted in `Untyped` and, ranked by name when some surface type has that member, in `untypedMembers` (a name only fallback ranking). Tiers per type follow section 5.9.5 (`ApiTiers`: A, B, C, S = supporting enum/struct/delegate/event arguments, U = unlisted class, counted as B; nested classes take the tier of their declaring type); a unit's tier is none, A, B or C (C when it has an error finding), `?` when unreadable.
- Findings: `error` = cannot run (Tier C): Windows libraries through ctypes (`windll`, `WinDLL`, `cdll.<windows library>`) or P/Invoke (ImplMap), `WndProc`/`DefWndProc` overrides, `Control.Handle`/`CreateParams`, `NativeWindow`/`Message`/`IMessageFilter`, the MSHTML document model (`HtmlDocument`, `WebBrowser.Document`), WPF namespaces and assemblies, COM interop assemblies and `ComImport` types, `Marshal` COM calls, registry and WMI types, Windows only Python modules (`win32api`, `_winreg`, `msvcrt` ...). The message is the section 5.9.6 sentence "Uses Windows-only API `x` at line N; this script cannot run on macOS". The same finding inside a platform check (`sys.platform`, `os.name`, names such as `IS_WINDOWS`) or a `try` that catches `ImportError` is a warning. `warning` also: Windows paths/environment variables/programs in string literals, `os.startfile`, `ctypes` in general, a reference to a Windows program assembly that is not shipped (`cYo.Common.Windows`), .NET Framework only assemblies (`System.Web.Extensions`, remoting, WCF ...), a bundled native Windows binary, `window.external.X` read as a property (section 5.9.8). `clr.AddReference*` names become `references` (kinds forms, drawing, core, core-missing, framework, framework-only, wpf, com, windows, bundled = a file of the same package, other).
- Bundled samples (`ComicRack/Output/Scripts`): 7 scripts, 5 tier A, 2 without WinForms; Button 3, Form 3, MessageBox 1, SaveFileDialog 1, DialogResult 5; `RefreshView.py` warns about `cYo.Common.Windows`. JSON is deterministic (schema 1, sorted, no times). Options: `fail-on-error` is a flag name of the shared `Options` parser.
- Public plugin checked (the one named in R11, downloaded as `mac/fixtures/plugins/bedetheque-scraper-2-v6.08/Bedetheque.Scraper.2_v6.08.crplugin`, git ignored, never run or unpacked; URL `https://github.com/maforget/Bedetheque-Scrapper-2/releases/download/v6.08/Bedetheque.Scraper.2_v6.08.crplugin`, SHA-256 `666016de2439ba179c27fb267b64d9896faa3bae71200f9b372969991954e5c5`, 13,282,162 bytes, GitHub reports no licence for the repository: the file stays local and only scan results may be committed): 32 Python files (one script with the UI, tier A/B types only, no tier C), 31 are bundled Python 2 standard library modules, plus `BedethequeFetcher.exe`, a 13 MB native Windows PyInstaller executable (not .NET): the script starts it as a local daemon when the file exists and otherwise looks for `BedethequeFetcher.py`, which the package does not contain. References: `System`, `System.Drawing`, `System.Windows.Forms`, `System.Web.Extensions` (framework-only: `JavaScriptSerializer`, which .NET 10 does not have). P4.17 has to decide how the layer answers these two (the daemon, `System.Web.Script.Serialization`).

**Facts from P4.6b (2026-10-27)**
- Corpus: `mac/scripting/fetch-corpus.sh` (`--list`, `--only NAME`, `--root DIR`; exit 3 = disk or 1.5 GB budget STOP) fetches 23 public plugins, each pinned (full commit hash for git sources, SHA-256 for release files) into `mac/fixtures/plugins/<name>/` with a `SOURCE.txt` (about 30 MB, git ignored); it refuses a root outside `mac/fixtures`, `mac/artifacts` or a temp folder (checked before anything is created), skips a folder that exists without `SOURCE.txt`, and runs nothing from the downloads (git only fetches one commit with hooks off). `mac/scripting/corpus.md` has URL, licence and pin of every source and what was not found: FrederikBaerentsen/ComicRack_Scripts, a "Comic Vine Scraper CE" fork (`cbanack/comic-vine-scraper` stands in) and CVIssueCount do not exist publicly; Stonepaw/ComicRack-Community-Scripts is a web site, not scripts, and was dropped.
- `mac/scripting/scan-corpus.sh [--fetch]` builds `crcli`, scans `mac/fixtures/plugins` and `ComicRack/Output/Scripts` with `--exclude-file mac/scripting/corpus-exclude.txt` and writes `mac/scripting/compat-usage.md` (committed) plus `mac/artifacts/script-corpus/scan.{json,txt}` (git ignored). Re-run it after a scanner change or a new pin; the report has no times and no machine paths. The code: `ScriptScanner.Exclude` / `ReadExcludeList` (paths relative to a scanned folder, case ignored, a folder takes its content with it), `UsageReport.Write` (`mac/ComicRack.Mac.Host/Scripting/Scan/UsageReport.cs`), `crcli script-scan --exclude-file F --markdown F`. Tests: Host.Tests `ScriptScanTests` (+4), Cli.Tests `ScriptScanCommandTests` (+1), `CorpusToolTests` (4; one of them is the R11 rule: no program code under `mac/` outside tests, tools, spikes, plans, scripting, fixtures names a corpus plugin).
- Scanner changes made for the corpus (P4.6a code, with tests): a `.crplugin` or `.zip` inside a plugin folder belongs to that folder (it used to be its own plugin named after the file); a string such as `www.example.com` is no longer a "Windows program" (`.com` counts only behind a backslash).
- Result (2026-10-27, scan of 29 plugins = 22 corpus plugins with scannable files + 7 bundled samples; 250 units: 219 Python, 12 assemblies, 7 HTML; reference pack 10.0.13, 1111 types): plugins by tier A 11, B 5, C 7, none 5, unreadable 1 (a Python 3 script); units A 78, B 17, C 9, none 145, unreadable 1. 95 units use the Forms or GDI+ surface: 284 types, 851 typed members. The top of the ranking is the dialog skeleton: `Size`/`Point` constructors, `Control.Size/Location/Controls/TabIndex/Name`, `Control.ControlCollection.Add`, `ButtonBase.Text`, `Label.Text`, `Button.DialogResult`, `DialogResult.OK/Cancel`, `MessageBox.Show`, `Control.Click`, `Form.ClientSize/StartPosition/FormBorderStyle/AcceptButton`, `SuspendLayout/ResumeLayout`; the first 100 ranked members run 16 percent of the 95 units completely, 300 members 44 percent, 500 members 74 percent (units whose typed members all lie among the first N). The long tail is real: P4.7 must generate the whole surface and P4.9 onward implements by this ranking.
- Tier C is rare and mostly third-party libraries a plugin carries (P/Invoke or registry declarations in `HtmlAgilityPack`, `Google.GData.Client`, `0Harmony`, `SQLiteFTS`, `MessageBoxManager`): 62 errors in 9 units of 7 plugins. Real Tier C reasons in plugin code: WPF (`PresentationFramework`, `XamlReader`) and `Control.Handle` in one plugin, `WndProc` overrides and `comctl32` image list calls in the Data Manager GUI assembly. Warnings: native Windows binaries (3 plugins), `System.Web*`/`System.ServiceModel` references (5 plugins), Windows paths in strings. 10871 accesses on values of unknown type (204 units) are in the name only table of section 6 of the report.
- The bundled-sample figures from P4.6a (7 scripts, 5 tier A) stay valid; Bedetheque Scraper 2 is tier A (one UI script, 43 Forms and GDI+ types) with warnings for the native daemon and `System.Web.Extensions`.

**Facts from P4.5 (2026-10-27)**
- Where it lives: `mac/ComicRack.Mac/Scripting/` has `ScriptCommandsViewModel` (`MainViewModel.Scripts`: the `Library`, `NewBooks`, `Books` and `Editor` command lists as `ScriptCommandItem`s, `CommandFor(item, hook)` for menu items, `RunAsync`, the hooks, `Console`), `AppScriptUi` (the app's `IScriptUi`, set on `host.Scripts.Ui` by `OnHostOpened`), `ReaderComicDisplay` (`ComicRack.ComicDisplay`), `UiCall`, `ScriptConsoleWindow`; the host side adds `ScriptRunContext`, `ScriptCommandRunner` and `ScriptConsoleLog` (`mac/ComicRack.Mac.Host/Scripting/`). `MainViewModel.Scripts.cs` wires it (`OpenScripts`, `RunScriptWithProgress`, `RunScriptAsync`, `CreateBookForFile`).
- A command runs on the thread pool, not on the UI thread: `ScriptCommandRunner.RunAsync(command, args, token)` never faults, it reports `ScriptRunOutcome.Completed|Failed|Cancelled` and unwraps the `TargetInvocationException` that `Command.Invoke` throws (the original showed that exception's text "Exception has been thrown by the target of an invocation."; the Mac alert shows the message of the script). The window runs it as a long operation (`RunExclusive`, the progress panel with the title "Automation '<name>'", the number of books and Cancel); the item commands are off while it runs (`IsBusy`).
- **Cancel is cooperative.** Nothing can stop a Python function from outside, so `print` (`TextWriterStream.Write`) and the calls of `ComicRack.App` that can loop (`AskQuestion`, `GetBook`, `GetLibraryBooks`, `GetComicPage`, `GetComicThumbnail`) raise `OperationCanceledException` once the run was cancelled (`ScriptRunContext`, an `AsyncLocal`). A script that never prints and never calls the host is not stopped: the result is given at once with `Detached = true` and the script ends by itself (a note goes to the console). The cancel is a property of the run, not of the app: it does not need a change of a script.
- Script threads reach the UI through `UiCall`: from a worker the work is posted and waited for; from the UI thread (the `Shutdown` hook, Editor scripts are not: they run on a worker) the work starts at once and `Dispatcher.PushFrame` runs a nested loop until it is done. **`UiCall.Run` is for asynchronous work and `UiCall.Invoke` for work that is done at once**: with one name a lambda that returns `null` is taken for the asynchronous overload and fails with a `NullReferenceException`.
- The hooks: `BookOpened` is raised by `ReaderManager.Open` (also for a book that is shown again, as `NavigatorManager.Open` does) and by `Adopt`; `ReaderResized` by the current tab's `ViewportSize` (whole units, not on a tab switch).
